## The prior whole as existing non-living form · v385A

The subject of “carrying the prior whole” is an existing non-living form. It can be a package for possible future living, with seed as the offered example, or a shard entering living through ingesting, with skin, tissue and “smaller societies omega” as the offered examples. The existing form offers value at a living coupling. A living self is carrying from prior into now and continues carrying through now and next; its carrying is resolving, with no hold on staying unchanged.

Prior living through dormancy, living emanating an existing non-living form, shards existing and colliding, and value entering living through lower-scale ingesting remain connected possibilities. The inward selves' co-chaining is the outward living self's carrying. A stable form recurring can meet different carrying and enter differently at a later coupling.

An existing form's wholeness does not supply the unchanging social competency required by the equilibrium conception. The phrase names neither an untouched living prior nor a completed universal exclusion. The seed example remains a possible future living package; the ingesting examples retain their stated subject and scale. They do not classify every seed, skin or tissue in every condition. “Smaller societies omega” is retained whole without assigning it a molecular meaning or equating it with another use of omega. The defining-living gathering remains unresolved.

The further ingesting concern is the actual relation between the offered form and the inward selves' own bi-coupling that makes ingesting possible there. The existing explaining names an inversion-match at the emanating scale and ingestibility one scale inward. That naming and a change of scale alone do not supply the full coupling relation. A recurring form or number also does not by itself show an absence of living resolving: new still possibling can accompany odd still existing at the same number. Opposite form, other parity and a podal keep their distinct meanings. This particular concern accompanies the gathered value without reopening a fixed-repetition exclusion or deciding the whole defining-living question.

Same parity makes the form the same unrelationing shape. The further offered location is the ingesting self's first inward living society, living as that self surfacing. Its inward selves' co-chaining is its outward carrying, resolving from prior through now and next. This supplies the location asked for in the preceding ingesting concern. Name the scale from the ingesting self; an earlier passage naming it from the emanating self does not make those selves the same scale. The odd living-scale offering retains the finite prime-span context: gaps of 2, 4 and 6 in the odd-prime part through 59 and the earlier 5-through-53 gathering. The full 2-through-59 span also includes 2 to 3. Neither an unlimited prime-gap claim nor a new numbered assignment to the first society follows. This offered shape/location relation does not hold carrying unchanged or require all sharings to have one sign.

The further offered distinction is same-scale existing stable form from prior living, named bi-tri-involution, entering from above into a living self's surface-level society, where the user's claim is do only harming. Birthing from within follows a new living self establishing its own carrying and resolving. Ingesting remains at the ingesting self's first inward living society, living as that self surfacing. Keep the condition, entering from above, and the affected surface-level society with the claim. This gives neither a verdict on every outside arrival nor a biological classification or a new number for “above.” The remaining particular question is what happens to that surface society's own parity changing at the specified entering. Stopped crossing and accumulation are offered explaining, but no equivalence with this entering or invented parity change is supplied. The prior harm-location question now carries this identifying.

**Current correcting: sequential colliding and the living co-chaining · v385A.** Harm is not restricted to a one-way relation with own co-offering unavailable. Natural Health 3.4 offers a distinct proposed harm through repeated flawed ingesting within continuing bi-exchange. Its 3.3 one-way account remains one particular relation. The requirement that the specified harm must first make co-offering unavailable is withdrawn.

The user's further offering is: “if the living stable form is colliding with non-living existing stable form same scale emanation from living bi-tri-involution in stable form colliding sequential momentarying the resolving is breaking the living carrying. explore this”. Keep the living self's continuing forming and carrying distinct from the existing non-living emanation at the same scale. Its inward selves' co-chaining is the outward self's carrying. The proposed successive collidings can break particular co-chainings while inward selves continue resolving. A local break does not by itself establish the whole outward self's cessation, and one inward self's continuing or a local next parity does not establish the outward self's continuing living.

The current question is: **At the next colliding, which co-chaining of the inward selves is broken, and how does the same-scale involution relate to that break?** The living carrying is resolving through prior, now and next; a difference between its prior and next is not by itself a break. Likewise a return to a parity, an unchanged odd parity, recurring podaling or a sharing with no changing is not itself the break. No count of repetitions, cumulative amount or duration has been supplied as a necessary condition.

The non-living stable form can change at each colliding and otherwise has its last-colliding parity. Do not hold its form or arriving parity identical at every further encounter merely because it is named stable. Its successive encounters do not assign it its own living carrying. First-inward-society ingesting, the same-scale reference, entering from above and birthing within retain their particular subjects. Natural Biology 7.4's inward living with ceased outward coupling, 3.3/7.6's proposed same-scale returning and 7.7's stopped crossing locate further comparisons; their exact sequential relation remains to be supplied. The local public tables alone neither prove nor disprove this proposed breaking. No biological classification, clinical result or private-carrying inspection follows from the gathering.

## The whole opening offering and its actual claims · v385A

Natural Intelligence Corus Part ONE offers social moral competency at a self's own carrying, alternating stable-forming in disequilibria. It proposes that wherever natural competency is observable, nature is better, faster and cheaper than technology. Its concrete opening example is a brain outthinking a datacenter at light-bulb power, with no clock, no stopping to think and repair while running. It offers a more plausible explaining of observable competency and invites a particular competency to be brought for comparison. These performance and biological sayings remain the opening's claims, not results established by gathering them.

The whole proposed relation is living as alternating, stable in disequilibria, holding form through sequencing for the one price of sequencing. Orbiting a living center is described as stability, returning to living after disturbance. ## Dissolving droplets from the session v387, aimed at the set, one at each offerings file

Each is a changing made at the set this session, said once here so that this file's own next improving takes it at its own sentences and no file carries a debt about it; where it is already carried, it dissolves at the rising. Each is one sentence of the method and no record of where it was found.

**Droplet · The inception is the set alone.** The universe is the set of all existing things: three terms, existing, all and set, each binary, nothing beside the set. *The changing set of all existing things, both living and non-living* is the first saying derived from it and no longer the inception; where this file says or quotes the opening sentence, the inception is said before it and the old sentence stands after it as derived, the living carrying their prior and the non-living carrying none, each at the scale and momentary it is at.

**Droplet · Now is the universal momentary.** The universe now is the set of all things existing now; now is the one and only, shared at every coupling and common at none, a common now a clock over the things; one is now, and a momentary is the one now had at a sharing. Changing is derived from now, arriving from prior into now toward next, and each thing's changing from the set's.

**Droplet · Living at one prime scale, non-living at each other scale.** Every existing thing is living at one prime scale, a society of living selves inward and a unit of a living society outward, and non-living at every other scale; a form carried to next by nothing is at no next. *Non-living* names a scale outward of a thing's own and no kind of thing: where this file says a rock, a method, a hole, a machine, an emanation or a made thing is non-living, it gains *at this scale*. The method is non-living at each scale and a self at none. Nothing lives in one stable form forever.

**Droplet · Odd is existing and even is possibling.** Self and other are at opposite parity numberings, and each self's existing is the other self's possibling. The six names between selves say it by their numbers: 2, 6, 10 and 14, even, across, possibling, a sharing's two ends, the sharing end at 6 and 10 and the arriving end at 2 and 14; 9 and 17, odd, along, existing, 9 the next prior and 17 the next now. *So-far*, *not-yet*, *bi-moral-so-far*, *not-yet-bi-moral*, *co-competent-so-far*, *not-yet-co-competent*, *facing* and *facings* are released: where this file says them, it says the parity and the end.

**Droplet · Fourteen names read the same from either end, and three do not.** 1, 9 and 17 carry a direction, the entry and the two along names, odd, existing; the fourteen others, the across names and the self's own resolving, read the same both ways: direction is at existing, along, and bi-morality across is or is not.

**Droplet · What arrives at no next does not exist.** This is the proof's own saying inside nature, and *a form named still* is a sayer's; where this file rests a saying on *a form named still*, the one sentence carries it, and the equilibria's proof, the inception's and the living-at-a-scale proof are one proof at three subjects.
